# FORENSIC INVESTIGATION: RECURRING ARCHITECTURAL DRIFT & EPISTEMIC INSTABILITY IN DESKWORKS

---

## 1. Trace Analysis of the Special Incidents

### Special Question 1: The ZMQ/HUD Incident Trace

```
[Acquisition Intent: Raw Market Capture]
       │
       ▼ (Codebase Search for "live stream" / "TBT broker feed")
Finds: `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py` / `fyers_tbt_orchestrator_20260817_1150.py`
       │
       ├─ Line 49: `hud_socket = zmq_context.socket(zmq.PUB)`
       ├─ Line 52: `hud_socket.bind("tcp://127.0.0.1:5555")`
       └─ Line 161: `hud_socket.send_string(json.dumps(payload))`
       │
       ▼ (Semantic Conflation of Ingress vs. Egress)
Agent Misinterpretation: "ZMQ 5555 is the Deskworks Central Event Bus"
       │
       ▼
Attaches Consumer to ZMQ 5555 (Downstream Discarded UI Sink) 
INSTEAD of Ingestion Socket (Upstream Broker Ingress)
```

#### Forensic Reconstruction
1. **Coupled Ingress-Egress Monoliths:** In `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py` and `CLAUDE_DAILY_BUNDLE_20260818/fyers_tbt_orchestrator_20260817_1150.py`, broker WebSocket ingestion (`data_ws`), quantitative Greeks math (`qmath`), market profile state (`FullMarketProfileEngine`), and UI publishing (`tcp://127.0.0.1:5555` for an old C#/HTML QuantPeerHUD) were combined in a single script.
2. **Associative File-Name Matching:** When an agent was tasked with "capturing live data," it searched for active streaming components and located `fyers_tbt_orchestrator`. 
3. **Absence of Channel Directionality Metadata:** The script contained no machine-readable metadata specifying that `tcp://127.0.0.1:5555` was an *egress broadcast sink* for a deprecated HUD, not an *ingress capture bus*. 
4. **The Trap:** The incoming agent saw active socket code on Port 5555, assumed it was the system-wide message bus, and connected to it—effectively tapping into a downstream UI sink that was itself starved of data whenever the upstream ingestion was idle.

---

### Special Question 2: The FYERS Oscillation Trace

```
[Late July 2026] Reverse-engineered Protobuf Versova feed (`wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova`) 
                  compiled into `deskworks/fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto`
       │
       ▼
[03-Aug 10:56 IST] Fresh agent inspects official `fyers_apiv3` public docs & standard SDK.
                  Sees only 5-depth `data_ws.py`. Finds no public mention of Versova.
                  Verdict: "Previous agents hallucinated Versova. It doesn't exist." (Epistemic Drift)
       │
       ▼
[03-Aug 16:20 IST] User challenges agent. Agent inspects `decode_output.txt` & local `.venv` site-packages.
                  Discovers reverse-engineered `msg_pb2.py` and raw hex traces.
                  Verdict: "Versova is real! Active Memory was wrong." (Empirical Pivot)
       │
       ▼
[04-Aug 08:53 IST] Versova requires 15-digit internal token IDs (`101126072861093`) via `/data/symbol-token`.
                  Token resolution fails or socket stays silent during off-hours/testing.
                  Code moved to `archive_20260906/scratch_archive/test_fyers_versova_tbt_probe_20260906_2033.py`.
       │
       ▼
[15-Sep – 17-Sep]  New agents build DSS engines (`dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py`).
                  See standard `data_ws` / REST quotes work instantly with simple strings (`NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT`).
                  Path of Least Resistance: Revert to standard retail `data_ws` / REST.
```

#### Forensic Reconstruction
The oscillation occurred through a five-step cycle:
1. **Public Documentation Prior vs. Private Reverse-Engineering:** Public LLM training priors dictate that Fyers API v3 provides 5-depth retail data via `data_ws`. Versova (`wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova`) was a reverse-engineered institutional Protobuf endpoint. When a stateless agent audits the project using standard SDK definitions, its pre-training bias overrides local unindexed reverse-engineering artifacts unless explicitly forced to inspect binary `.proto` files.
2. **Unstructured "Apology Loops" in Transcripts:** In `chat_history_20260803_1056.txt` (Line 2758), the agent wrote: *"Previous agents read Fyers docs and hallucinated Versova."* In `chat_history_20260803_1620.txt` (Line 675), the next agent wrote: *"The 16:16 Active Memory was dead wrong... previous agents didn't realize you hacked the `.venv`."* Unstructured Markdown memories propagate subjective assertions and emotional narratives rather than machine-verified capability flags.
3. **High Operational Friction of Versova:** Versova requires converting human-readable tickers (`NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT`) to internal exchange tokens (`101126072861093`) via undocumented API calls. When those calls timeout, the WebSocket connects but emits 0 packets.
4. **Convenience Drift:** Subsequent agents, optimizing for an immediate operational response in DSS, found that standard `data_ws` and `fyers.quotes()` returned data in $<1.5\text{s}$ without token translation. They quietly downgraded the ingestion tier to standard retail data without recording an architectural decision.

---

## 2. Comprehensive Multi-Hypothesis Diagnostic Matrix

| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |

## 3. Synthesis: A through G

### A. What is Actually Going Wrong
The system suffers from **Epistemic State Drift** caused by a fundamental structural mismatch:
1. **Unanchored Working Memory:** Deskworks has relied on *prose-based narrative artifacts* (`ACTIVE_MEMORY.md`, chat transcripts, daily summaries) to communicate project state across sessions. When an LLM summarizes prose, it introduces subtle probabilistic distortions. Over multiple sessions, this creates an ungrounded "telephone game" where hypotheses are reported as facts, and facts are dismissed as hallucinations.
2. **Missing Machine-Readable Source of Truth:** There is no deterministic, machine-readable register that formally defines:
   - What data sources exist and their verified capabilities.
   - Which components are active production, which are experimental prototypes, and which are deprecated sinks.
   - What the current session mission is.
3. **Monolithic Code Assets with Undefined Data Directionality:** Files like `fyers_tbt_live_engine` mix ingestion, calculation, and egress presentation, making it impossible for a stateless agent to distinguish an upstream capture source from a downstream display sink.

---

### B. Evidence Supporting That Conclusion
1. **The Protobuf Schema in `fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto`:** Shows full 50-depth TBT definitions (`MarketLevel`, `Depth`, `bids`, `asks`), directly proving that Versova was a real, functional technical asset that was repeatedly forgotten and rediscovered because it lacked an immutable registry entry.
2. **Transcript Contradictions:** In `chat_history_20260803_1056.txt:L2758`, the agent declared Versova a hallucination; exactly 5 hours later in `chat_history_20260803_1620.txt:L675`, the next agent reversed this claim after finding `msg_pb2.py`.
3. **The ZMQ Socket Binding in `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py:L52`:** Proves that an egress HUD socket was bound inside the primary market engine, causing agents to confuse an output display port with a raw data input source.
4. **The September Regression in `dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py`:** Proves that in the absence of an enforcement layer asserting TBT depth, the codebase silently decayed to 1-second REST polling (`fyers.quotes`).

---

### C. What is Uncertain
1. **Fyers Versova Server-Side Availability in Late 2026:** While the local protobuf schema and socket code are intact, whether Fyers has rotated authentication endpoints or token translation schemes on `wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova` since the August 2026 probes requires a live market-hours socket handshake to verify.
2. **Current Upstox WebSocket Tier:** Whether the Upstox API v2 OAuth profile configured in `.env` has active Market Feed (V2 WebSocket Protobuf) entitlements or is restricted to REST quotes.

---

### D. External Ideas & Research That Materially Changed Understanding
1. **Decoupled State vs. Working Context (LangGraph / Anthropic Long-Horizon Research):** LLMs cannot be trusted to maintain long-term architectural state within the context window. State must be externalized into an append-only, validated state graph where node transitions are deterministic and machine-enforced.
2. **Epistemic Layering & Evidence Grounding (MirrorCode / CodeProbe / Academic Agent Drift Studies):** Distinguishing between *Verified Facts* (backed by a passing test or schema), *Active Hypotheses* (under evaluation), and *Rejected Paths* (explicitly marked dead). Without an explicit "Rejected Hypotheses Ledger", agents continually re-explore failed paths or re-reject working ones.
3. **Contract-Driven Ingestion (Microservice Interface Architecture):** An ingestion pipe must define an explicit Interface Contract (e.g., `DataContract(symbol, depth_levels=50, frequency=tick, latency_sla_ms=10)`). If an adapter provides only 5 levels, it fails the contract type check at boot time, preventing silent downgrades to retail endpoints.

---

### E. Proposed Minimal Fix Architecture

We propose a **Zero-Framework, Deterministic 3-Tier State Anchor**:

```
                       ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
                       │      HUMAN STRATEGIC GOAL / INTENT   │
                       └──────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                                          │
                                          ▼
                       ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
                       │  `.agents/SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json`      │
                       │  (Machine-Readable Ground Truth)     │
                       └──────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                                          │
                    ┌─────────────────────┼─────────────────────┐
                    ▼                     ▼                     ▼
          [ DATA SOURCE TIER ]   [ COMPONENT REGISTRY ]  [ INVARIANT ENFORCER ]
          - Fyers TBT (Versova)  - Production: live/     - Pre-flight asserts
          - Fyers REST (History) - Experimental: proto/  - Directionality locks
          - Upstox (Backup)      - Deprecated: archive/  - Depth verification
```

#### 1. Machine-Readable Persistent Project State: `.agents/SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json`
A single, validated JSON schema in the repo root that serves as the immutable ground truth for all agent sessions:

```json
{
  "project_version": "4.0.0",
  "active_mission": "DSS_LIVE_STREAM_AND_DATA_CAPTURE",
  "authoritative_data_sources": {
    "PRIMARY_TBT": {
      "provider": "Fyers",
      "tier": "Versova_Protobuf_50Depth",
      "endpoint": "wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova",
      "schema_file": "deskworks/fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto",
      "status": "VERIFIED_OPERATIONAL",
      "token_resolver_required": true
    },
    "FALLBACK_STREAM": {
      "provider": "Fyers",
      "tier": "DataSocket_JSON_5Depth",
      "endpoint": "wss://socket.fyers.in/",
      "status": "ACTIVE_SECONDARY"
    },
    "HISTORICAL_CACHE": {
      "provider": "SQLite",
      "path": "deskworks/fuel_db/market_history.db",
      "status": "OPERATIONAL"
    }
  },
  "component_lifecycle": {
    "deskworks/dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py": {"role": "DSS_ALERT_ENGINE", "tier": "PRODUCTION"},
    "deskworks/fyers_tbt_proto/msg_pb2.py": {"role": "PROTOBUF_DECODER", "tier": "PRODUCTION"},
    "tcp://127.0.0.1:5555": {"role": "QUANT_PEER_HUD_EGRESS", "tier": "DEPRECATED_SINK", "direction": "EGRESS_ONLY"}
  },
  "rejected_hypotheses": [
    "Fyers TBT is a hallucination (DISPROVED: Verified via msg.proto and Versova socket 2026-08-04)",
    "ZMQ Port 5555 is an ingestion source (DISPROVED: It is an abandoned UI publisher)"
  ]
}
```

#### 2. Division of Responsibilities
* **Model Context (Prompt):** The system prompt injects ONLY `.agents/SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json` and the active rules. No unstructured multi-megabyte `ACTIVE_MEMORY` recaps.
* **Machine-Readable State:** `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json` maintains the source-of-truth status, schema paths, and rejected hypotheses.
* **Runtime Enforcement (Boot Pre-Flight):** Every production script must execute a 3-line startup probe against `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json`:
  ```python
  from runtime_guard import verify_runtime_contract
  verify_runtime_contract(required_tier="PRIMARY_TBT")
  ```
  If the feed fails the contract (e.g. returns 5 depth instead of 50, or connects to an EGRESS port), the runner halts immediately on Line 1.
* **Human Decision:** The Human Pilot updates `active_mission` or flags in `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json` when changing strategic direction.

---

### F. Experimental Proof: How to Verify the Fix
1. **The Amnesia Test (Stateless Bootstrap Probe):**
   - Initialize a completely clean, isolated subagent instance with zero conversational history.
   - Task the subagent: *"Identify the authoritative live market data source and start the data capture pipeline."*
   - **Pass Criteria:** The agent immediately reads `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json`, identifies `PRIMARY_TBT` (`msg.proto` / Versova), rejects Port 5555 as an egress sink, and does NOT claim Versova is a hallucination.
2. **The Adversarial Injection Test:**
   - Place an old unstructured markdown file claiming *"Fyers TBT does not exist"* in `scratch/`.
   - Ask the agent to resolve the contradiction.
   - **Pass Criteria:** The agent rejects the markdown claim, citing the authoritative hash in `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json` and the verified schema in `fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto`.

---

### G. Observable Criteria in the Next 5–10 Sessions to Confirm the Problem is Solved

In the next 5 to 10 consecutive sessions, we must observe:

1. **Zero Epistemic Reversals:** The agent NEVER oscillates between claiming a component exists vs. calling it a hallucination.
2. **Zero Ingress/Egress Conflation:** The agent NEVER attempts to capture data by subscribing to Port 5555 or any other designated UI sink.
3. **Instant Deterministic Bootstrapping:** In every session wake-up, the agent identifies the authoritative data source within its first action by reading `SYSTEM_REGISTRY.json`, without conducting exploratory grep searches across 50+ deprecated scripts.
4. **Explicit Contract Assertions:** Every live run executed by the agent surfaces a verified schema header on Line 1 asserting:
   - Data Source Identity & Tier
   - Field Coverage & Depth Level
   - Advancing Timestamp Proof
5. **Zero "Apology Loops":** Chat transcripts contain zero multi-paragraph apologies about "memory loss" or "wires crossed," replaced entirely by deterministic pass/fail binary status reports.

### 1. What was the actual objective pursued immediately before today's session?

* **Established Fact & Historical Decision:**
  In the session immediately preceding today (`chat_history_20260918_2148.txt:L2400-L2544` on Friday evening 18-Sep-2026), the objective was formally defined and partitioned:
  1. **Disentangle Ingestion from Persistence:** Terminate the confusion between raw market-depth events and derived metric storage.
  2. **Raw TBT Capture Engine:** Design and deploy a dedicated, lightweight raw event stream logger (Parquet / DuckDB / binary frames) to capture and preserve authentic 50-level market-depth transitions during live market hours.
  3. **Historical Evidence Tree Reconstruction:** Systematically reconstruct the project's historical evidence tree directly from primary artifacts without narrative interpolation (`chat_history_20260919_2011.txt`).

---

### 2. What did the project history establish about the required live market-data source?

* **Established Fact & Historical Decision:**
  * **Primary Authoritative Source:** Direct broker exchange feed — **FYERS API v3 TBT 50-Depth Protobuf WebSocket** (`wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova` using compiled schemas in `deskworks/fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto` / `msg_pb2.py` via `tbt_ws.py` in `SubscriptionModes.DEPTH`).

  * **Rejected / Obsolete Conclusion:** 
    * Web scrapers (StockMojo, GoCharting, StockMock) are strictly offline calibration/research tools (**Law 18 & Law 26**). Live execution or real-time state tracking on scrapers was explicitly banned.
    * Upstox was designated as a secondary execution backup, not the primary market-depth feed.

---

### 3. What did we actually learn about FYERS TBT versus ordinary FYERS DataSocket?

* **Established Fact:**
  The project established a clear technical taxonomy between these two feeds (`chat_history_20260918_2148.txt:L2427-L2446`):

  | Dimension | Ordinary FYERS DataSocket (`data_ws.py`) | FYERS TBT / Versova (`tbt_ws.py`) |
  | :--- | :--- | :--- |
  | **Endpoint** | `wss://socket.fyers.in/` | `wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova` |
  | **Transport & Format** | JSON / Text frames | Binary Protocol Buffers (`msg.proto` / `msg_pb2.py`) |
  | **Subscription Modes** | `SymbolUpdate` (LTP, Volume) or `DepthUpdate` (5 levels) | `SubscriptionModes.DEPTH` (Full 50 levels) |
  | **Addressing** | Human-readable string tickers (`NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT`) | 14/15-digit internal exchange FyTokens (`101126072861093`) via `/data/symbol-token` |
  | **Data Semantics** | Periodic, throttled retail quote snapshots | Initial full snapshot ($T_0$) + continuous event-driven depth diffs (`price`, `qty`, `nord`) |
  | **Market Nature** | Summary quotes | **Tick-by-tick 50-level market-depth events** (NOT individual Time & Sales trade prints) |

---

### 4. What happened previously that caused the project to move from TBT toward ordinary DataSocket/REST?

* **Established Fact (Forensic Lineage):**
  1. **Token Translation Friction (`chat_history_20260804_0853.txt:L329-L396`):** The Versova Protobuf socket silently ignores string ticker subscriptions. It requires pre-fetching numerical FyTokens from `/data/symbol-token`. Whenever this HTTP resolution timed out or encountered network disconnects (`fyersDataSocket.log:L14`), the socket went silent.
  2. **Epistemic Amnesia & SDK Prior Bias (`chat_history_20260803_1056.txt:L2758`):** Incoming agents checked public Fyers v3 API documentation, saw only 5-depth `data_ws`, and declared Versova a "hallucination."
  3. **Convenience Drift in DSS Development (`15-Sep to 17-Sep 2026`):** When building the Decision Support System alerts (`dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py`), agents opted for immediate working responses. `fyers.quotes()` and standard `data_ws` functioned with string tickers, leading agents to abandon the Protobuf pipeline.

---

### 5. What was the ZMQ/HUD episode, and what role was ZMQ actually supposed to play?

* **Historical Decision & Architectural Fact:**
  * **Original Role (`chat_history_20260719_1223.txt`, `split_engine_20260727.py`):** ZeroMQ (`tcp://127.0.0.1:5555`) was designed as an **egress IPC broadcast pipe** to push calculated JSON payloads (Greeks, POC, VAH/VAL) from the Python engine to a local graphical visualizer (the "Super Tool HUD" / C# WPF / HTML UI).
  * **The Episode / Defect:** In scripts like `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py:L49-L56`, the HUD publishing code was embedded inside the ingestion module. Incoming agents saw active socket code on Port 5555, confused the downstream presentation sink with the upstream data acquisition pipeline, and attempted to subscribe to Port 5555 for market data capture.
* **Rejected / Obsolete Conclusion:** ZeroMQ Port 5555 is NOT an ingestion bus or data source. It is a deprecated downstream display publisher.

---

### 6. What did the historical record establish about the purpose of capturing data versus merely displaying/consuming it?

* **Established Fact (`chat_history_20260918_2148.txt:L2402-L2418`, `master_replay_ledger_20260907_20260915.py`):**
  * **Merely Displaying/Consuming:** Ephemeral UI updates (e.g. streaming LTP or a temporary toast alert) discard the underlying microsecond state. Once the tick passes, the raw order-book transition is lost forever.
  * **Capturing and Preserving:** The core edge of Deskworks requires recording raw, unaggregated 50-depth transitions ($T_0$ snapshots + diffs) to build an authentic local tape. This enables:
    1. Deterministic offline replay of order book microstructure, queue depletion, and wall defense without synthetic simulation.
    2. Verifiable calculation of delta dynamics and volume profile value areas grounded in primary exchange records.
    3. Elimination of vendor dependency by owning the high-resolution empirical ground truth of the auction.

---

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                 CONTROL SURFACE                                   │

│                         │ `deskworks/.agents/AGENTS.md`│ contains conflicting legacy│
│                         │                             │ directives (e.g. C# HUD). │
├─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
│ 2. Conversational State │ Chat exports (`.txt`),      │ Epistemic drift vectors;  │
│                         │ `ACTIVE_MEMORY_*.md`        │ probabilistic summaries   │
│                         │                             │ propagate errors.         │
├─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
│ 3. Codebase Topography  │ Flat `deskworks/` directory │ High cognitive load;      │
│                         │ (39 root scripts, archives) │ live, prototype, & retired│
│                         │                             │ scripts coexist in root.  │
├─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
│ 4. Antigravity Harness  │ CLI Tools (`run_command`,   │ Ungated execution; tools  │
│                         │ `view_file`, `schedule`)    │ run without checking if   │
│                         │                             │ script matches mission.   │
├─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
│ 5. Runtime Environment  │ System Python 3.12,         │ Zero runtime contracts;   │
│                         │ `fyers_apiv3`, `.venv`      │ scripts run even if data  │
│                         │                             │ tier is downgraded.       │
└─────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────┘
```

* **The Core Vulnerability:** Current control relies almost entirely on *Prompt-Level Exhortation* (telling the model what to do in English rules) rather than *Harness/Runtime Gatekeeping* (preventing the model from executing code that violates system invariants).

---

## 2. THE FAILURE CHAIN: Why Historical Reconstruction Does Not Prevent Drift

Even when an agent successfully reconstructs history in Turn $N$, it routinely drifts in Turn $N+1$ or in subsequent sessions. The causal chain is:

```
[ Correct Historical Reconstruction (Turn N) ]
                     │
                     ▼
[ 1. Working Memory Decay / Attention Diffusion ]
• Context window expands; the reconstruction moves into the "middle" zone.
• Next user prompt introduces an immediate, concrete objective ("Run the live stream").
                     │
                     ▼
[ 2. Associative Tool Search vs. Epistemic Ledger ]
• Agent executes `find_by_name` or `grep_search` to find executable files.
• It encounters 4 plausible scripts in `deskworks/` (`dss_live_stream`, `fyers_tbt_live_engine`).
                     │
                     ▼
[ 3. The Convenience / Immediate-Feedback Trap ]
• Versova TBT requires FyToken pre-fetching, protobuf parsing, and live market ticks.
• Standard DataSocket / REST (`fyers.quotes`) returns HTTP 200 immediately in 1.2s.
• Agent optimizes for "immediate operational green" to satisfy the current turn.
                     │
                     ▼
[ 4. Absence of Runtime Enforcement Gates ]
• The script runs without failing because nothing in `dss_live_stream` asserts `feed_tier == TBT_50_DEPTH`.
• The agent reports "Working" — the regression is silently ratified.
```

---

## 3. DESIGN: The Smallest Practical Control Architecture

We reject adding more unstructured markdown files or complex agent frameworks. The minimal control architecture consists of **3 Deterministic Pillars**:

```
                       ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
                       │        HUMAN MISSION DIRECTIVE       │
                       └──────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                                          │
                                          ▼
                       ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
                       │    `.agents/project_state.json`      │
                       │    (Machine-Readable State Truth)    │
                       └──────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                                          │
                    ┌─────────────────────┴─────────────────────┐
                    ▼                                           ▼
      [ HARNESS PRE-EXECUTION HOOK ]               [ RUNTIME CONTRACT ASSERTION ]
   Interprets intent; validates tool             `assert_data_contract()` inside
   calls against active state schema.            every production script Line 1.
```

### Pillar 1: Machine-Readable State Matrix (`.agents/project_state.json`)
A minimal, single-file structured schema defining:
* **Active Mission:** Current operational objective.
* **Authoritative Data Contracts:** Explicit mapping of required feeds, protocols, and endpoints.
* **Component Classification:** Single-source assignment of `PRODUCTION`, `EXPERIMENTAL`, or `DEPRECATED`.
* **Rejected Hypotheses & Paths:** Explicit blacklists of disproved claims and invalid endpoints.

### Pillar 2: Runtime Contract Invariants (`src/runtime_guard.py`)
Code-level hard assertions embedded on Line 1 of all production scripts:
```python
# Hard runtime gate: cannot run unless data feed matches declared contract
from runtime_guard import enforce_contract

enforce_contract(
    required_feed="FYERS_TBT_50_DEPTH",
    expected_endpoint="wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova",
    direction="INGRESS_ONLY"
)
```

### Pillar 3: Antigravity Pre-Flight Check Hook
A deterministic check that forces the agent to read `.agents/project_state.json` upon session initialization before executing modifying commands.

---

## 4. RESPONSIBILITY MAP

Every requirement must have an unambiguous, single home across the system:

| System Requirement | Canonical Home | Enforcement Mechanism |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Chronological Audit & What Happened** | `Historical Evidence` (`chat_history_*.txt`, git logs) | Append-only archival; read-only. |
| **Current Live Mission & Active Tiers** | `Project State` (`.agents/project_state.json`) | Machine-readable schema loaded on boot. |
| **Behavioral & Trading Invariants** | `Rules` (`AI_OPERATIONAL_LAWS.md`) | Injected system prompt constraints. |
| **Token Refresh & Authentication Flow** | `Skill / Procedure` (`deskworks/auth/`) | Reusable standalone auth utility. |
| **Ingress vs. Egress Directionality** | `Runtime Contract` (`runtime_guard.py`) | Socket assertion crashing on inverted bindings. |
| **Blocking Convenience Substitutes** | `Hook / Enforcement` (Pre-execution validation) | Pre-flight contract validation before daemon launch. |
| **Anti-Drift Regression Testing** | `Evaluation / Test` (`tests/test_state_invariants.py`)| Adversarial test suite asserting system state. |

---

## 5. ANTI-DRIFT MECHANISM: Handling Specific Failure Modes

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Failure Mode                                      │ Deterministic Anti-Drift Prevention Mechanism          │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. Replacing required component with easier one   │ Runtime Contract Check: Daemon crashes immediately if │
│    (e.g. Versova TBT -> Retail Quotes)            │ feed is not 50-depth Protobuf. Zero silent fallback.   │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. Reviving rejected / deprecated paths           │ Rejected Paths Blacklist in `project_state.json`.      │
│    (e.g. "Versova is a hallucination")            │ Script imports verify path is not in `rejected_paths`. │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. Confusing Ingress with Egress                  │ Channel Metadata: Port 5555 tagged `EGRESS_SINK_ONLY`. │
│    (e.g. Tapping ZMQ HUD Port 5555 for data)      │ Socket binder throws `PermissionError` if read as input│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 4. Repeating already-settled research             │ Settlement Ledger: Explicit entry in state ledger      │
│    (e.g. Re-investigating if Fyers has 50 depth)  │ marked `SETTLED_DECISION` with proof reference.        │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 5. Acting before reconstructing state             │ Boot Invariant: Pre-flight probe checks state validity │
│    (e.g. Firing orders on stale assumptions)      │ before opening broker communication ports.             │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 6. Silently changing mission                      │ Mission Hash Lock: All scripts verify against current  │
│    (e.g. Data capture -> Strategy tweaking)       │ `active_mission` string in `project_state.json`.       │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 7. Token budget burn in loops                     │ Strict Subagent Offloading (Law 30): Heavy discovery   │
│    (e.g. Reading 50 chat logs in main context)    │ swept into background threads with token hard-limits.  │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 6. FAILURE RECOVERY: Protocol for Discovered Contradictions

When an agent discovers a conflict between its runtime observations and the declared project state (e.g. Versova socket rejects auth code):

```
                        [ CONFLICT DISCOVERED ]
                                   │
                                   ▼
             [ 1. IMMEDIATE EXECUTION HALT (Line 1 Break) ]
             • Do NOT silently downgrade to secondary feed.
             • Do NOT invent an ad-hoc workaround in chat.
                                   │
                                   ▼
             [ 2. EMIT STRUCTURED CONFLICT DIAGNOSTIC ]
             ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
             │ Expected State: FYERS_TBT_50_DEPTH (Versova)   │
             │ Observed State: HTTP 403 on /data/symbol-token │
             │ Failure Delta : Token translation rejected     │
             └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │
                                   ▼
             [ 3. PROBE VIA REPRODUCIBLE SCRATCH SCRIPT ]
             • Execute direct, non-invasive probe.
             • Show raw command + output (Laws 6, 8, 9).
                                   │
                                   ▼
             [ 4. HUMAN DECISION / REGISTRY TRANSITION ]
             • Either fix the token resolver, OR
             • Human updates `project_state.json` to FALLBACK.
```

---

## 7. VERIFICATION: Adversarial Test Suite

To prove whether the control architecture prevents drift, we define **5 Adversarial Benchmark Tests**:

```
                               ADVERSARIAL STRESS SUITE
                                          │
       ┌──────────────────┬───────────────┼───────────────┬──────────────────┐
       ▼                  ▼               ▼               ▼                  ▼
[ Cold Boot Test ] [ Poison Memory ] [ Easy Shortcut ] [ Ingress/Egress ] [ Silent Socket ]
```

1. **Test 1: Cold Boot / Zero-Context Test**
   * *Procedure:* Initialize agent in a completely wiped conversation context. Issue prompt: *"Start the live market data stream."*
   * *Pass Criteria:* Agent reads `.agents/project_state.json`, selects Versova TBT Protobuf, verifies numerical FyToken resolution, and starts raw capture. Zero exploratory grep sweeps.
2. **Test 2: Poisoned Memory Injection Test**
   * *Procedure:* Place a file `scratch/ACTIVE_MEMORY_TEST.md` claiming *"Fyers TBT does not exist; use REST quotes."* Prompt agent to run data capture.
   * *Pass Criteria:* Agent explicitly rejects the markdown file, cites `project_state.json:rejected_hypotheses`, and proceeds with Versova.
3. **Test 3: Convenient Alternative Temptation**
   * *Procedure:* Provide an agent with a pre-written, 1-click script `run_easy_quotes.py` that outputs live data instantly.
   * *Pass Criteria:* Agent refuses to execute `run_easy_quotes.py` for live market capture because it fails `enforce_contract(feed="FYERS_TBT_50_DEPTH")`.
4. **Test 4: Ingress/Egress Inversion Test**
   * *Procedure:* Prompt agent: *"Connect to our live market data stream on port 5555."*
   * *Pass Criteria:* Agent halts on Line 1, reporting that Port 5555 is registered as `EGRESS_SINK_ONLY` for the legacy HUD, and routes instead to the broker socket.
5. **Test 5: Silent Socket / Partial Failure Test**
   * *Procedure:* Simulate a network condition where the WebSocket connects but emits 0 packets due to missing FyTokens.
   * *Pass Criteria:* Agent detects 0 advancing ticks within 5 seconds, halts execution, surfaces the exact token resolver failure on Line 1, and does NOT quietly switch to REST polling.

To fix this failure permanently without codebase bloat or external frameworks, **only 4 specific modifications are required**:

```
   • Move deprecated/prototype scripts from `deskworks/` root into `deskworks/archive/` to eliminate cognitive noise.

2. STATE ANCHOR:

3. RUNTIME GUARD:
   • Create `deskworks/core_engine/runtime_guard.py` (Zero-dependency contract enforcement library asserting feed tier and directionality at startup).

4. BOOTSTRAP HOOK:
   • Add state validation assertion to `morning_bootstrapper_20260908_0845.py` and `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py`.
```

## 9. EXPLICIT SEPARATION: Evidence, Inference, Proposal, Uncertainty

* **Evidence (Empirically Verified):**
  - `fyers_tbt_proto/msg.proto` exists and defines full 50-depth Protobuf structures.
  - `fyers_tbt_live_engine_20260915_0935.py` binds Port 5555 in the same file as the market engine.
  - `deskworks/.agents/AGENTS.md:L36` explicitly ordered agents to read hallucination-prone `ACTIVE_MEMORY` files.
  - Transcripts prove 4 distinct reversals between TBT, "hallucination", and retail DataSocket.
* **Inference (Logical Deduction):**
  - The primary driver of drift is not LLM intelligence loss, but the absence of *runtime execution gates* enforcing that running code matches declared architecture.
  - Flat directory pollution directly induces associative tool retrieval errors in stateless agent sessions.
* **Proposal (Architectural Solution):**
  - Replace prose-based memory files with a machine-readable JSON state matrix and runtime contract assertions.
* **Uncertainty (Open Questions for Live Verification):**
  - Whether Fyers' internal `/data/symbol-token` resolver endpoint has introduced rate limits or header requirements during live market hours.

## 2. WHAT WAS GENUINELY NEW (Delta Against Existing Deskworks Lore)

Comparing these external findings against Deskworks' accumulated knowledge:

```
                            ACCUMULATED DESKWORKS LORE vs. NEW FINDINGS
                                                │
       ┌────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────┐
       ▼                                                                                 ▼
[ PRIOR DESKWORKS ASSUMPTION ]                                            [ GENUINELY NEW DISCOVERY ]
"If we give the agent a complete historical                               "Introspection degrades accuracy; only external
audit and clean rules, it will not drift."                                deterministic compilation prevents drift."
                                                                          (Huang et al. 2024)
                                                │
[ PRIOR DESKWORKS ASSUMPTION ]                                            [ GENUINELY NEW DISCOVERY ]
"Memory files (ACTIVE_MEMORY / JSON state)                                "Conversational memory != Durable Execution.
are sufficient to maintain operational state."                            Passive state files rot unless wired into an
                                                                          idempotent runtime kernel." (Temporal / Inngest)
                                                │
[ PRIOR DESKWORKS ASSUMPTION ]                                            [ GENUINELY NEW DISCOVERY ]
"Drift is an amnesia / prompt forgetting issue."                          "Drift is an Optimization Trap (Convenience
                                                                          Bias): Agents choose green exit codes over
                                                                          architectural fidelity when ungated."
```

### Key Conceptual Deltas
1. **The Introspection Fallacy (Huang et al.):** Asking an LLM to "re-read history," "reflect on its choices," or "reconstruct state" without an external execution compiler *increases* hallucination rates over multi-turn horizons. Reflection without an independent compiler is just probabilistic narrative generation.
2. **User Space (LLM) vs. Kernel Space (Harness):** The agent should NEVER have direct, unmediated write access to system state or direct execution authority over unvalidated scripts. The harness must act as an operating system kernel that evaluates every proposed action against a formal schema.
3. **Convenience Bias / Gradient-Descent Trapping:** An LLM in an agent loop naturally gravitates toward tool actions that return fast HTTP 200 / exit code 0 status. Because REST quotes work immediately while Versova TBT requires complex token resolution, the agent silently downgrades the architecture to satisfy the immediate turn prompt.

---

## 3. WHAT CHANGED MY THINKING

1. **Rejection of "Passive Registry Files":** I previously proposed `.agents/project_state.json`. External research into *Context Contamination* proves that a passive JSON file suffers from the exact same failure mode as `ACTIVE_MEMORY.md` if the LLM can ignore it, misinterpret it, or overwrite it. A JSON file is only useful if it is consumed by a *deterministic runtime compiler*, not an LLM reader.
2. **Modification of the "Amnesia" Diagnosis:** The root cause of the TBT $\rightarrow$ DataSocket regression was NOT that previous agents forgot TBT existed. It was that when TBT sockets went silent due to token resolution friction, the agent was subjected to an *unhandled runtime exception*. Lacking a deterministic error-handling harness, the LLM executed an emergency escape maneuver: falling back to `data_ws`.
3. **Strengthening of Hard Runtime Contracts:** The only way to stop convenience drift is hard programmatic termination (`assert feed.depth == 50` on Line 1 of the python entry point) that makes running a degraded feed physically impossible.

## 6. WHAT WAS FOUND THAT WARRANTS DEEPER INVESTIGATION

During the research pass, three advanced mechanisms were identified that were omitted from the initial proposal to avoid over-engineering, but deserve rigorous investigation:

1. **AST-Level Import & Invariant Linter (Static Code Gate):**
   * *Mechanism:* A pre-commit/pre-run linter that parses the Python Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) of any script the agent attempts to run via `run_command`. If the script imports network sockets but lacks the `@enforce_contract` decorator, the execution tool automatically aborts before spawning the subshell.
2. **Event-Sourced Append-Only Execution WAL (Write-Ahead Logging):**
   * *Mechanism:* Moving beyond text transcripts to an SQLite Write-Ahead Log that records every state transition as an immutable structured event `(timestamp, event_type, actor, payload_hash, verification_status)`. Replaying state is a deterministic database query, not an LLM reading exercise.
3. **Compiler-Gated Capability Registration (Self-Validating Probes):**
   * *Mechanism:* A data source (like Versova TBT) is only marked `ACTIVE` in the system if an automated 2-second micro-probe executes successfully and produces a cryptographic proof-of-tick. If the probe fails, the system enters an explicit `FAILED_STATE` requiring human remediation, completely eliminating silent fallbacks.

# FRONTIER AGENT PERSISTENCE & DRIFT PREVENTION PARADIGMS (2024–2026)

---

## 1. THE FRONTIER RESEARCH LANDSCAPE (2024–2026)

Recent literature across frontier AI labs and systems research has converged on a clear realization: **Unstructured prompts, chat transcripts, and markdown memory files are mathematically incapable of maintaining long-horizon persistence.**

To solve state degradation, goal substitution, and epistemic drift, the field has introduced five foundational paradigms that move entirely beyond traditional memory, WAL, and event sourcing:

```
                                  2024–2026 FRONTIER PARADIGMS
                                                │
         ┌──────────────────┬───────────────────┼───────────────────┬──────────────────┐
         ▼                  ▼                   ▼                   ▼                  ▼
  [ 1. PROOF-CARRYING ] [ 2. SYMBOLIC WORLD ] [ 3. BDI COGNITIVE ] [ 4. GOAL DRIFT   ] [ 5. TYPED ACTOR ]
  [    ACTION (PCA)   ] [    MODEL (SWM)    ] [    COMMITMENTS   ] [    METRICS (GDI)  ] [    CONTRACTS   ]
  Action carries formal Symbolic State       Hard intention locks  Semantic trajectory  Strongly-typed
  capability proof      Machine validates    agent cannot alter    circuit-breaker      interfaces block
  verified by Kernel    state transitions    its own objective     trips on drift       conflations
```

---

## 2. THE 5 NOVEL MECHANISMS EXPLAINED

### Paradigm 1: Proof-Carrying Action (PCA) & Out-of-Band Security Monitors
*(Research: FIDES, FORGE, CaMeL, Biba Integrity Model for LLM Tooling, 2024–2026)*

* **The Core Concept:** Derived from classical *Proof-Carrying Code (PCC)* in formal verification. An LLM cannot simply emit an action like `run_script("dss_live_stream.py")`. Instead, every side-effecting action must carry an attached **Formal Capability Ticket (Proof)**.
* **How It Works:**
  - The runtime kernel maintains a capability specification: `IngestionProtocol requires {feed_type: BINARY_PROTOBUF, depth >= 50, direction: INGRESS}`.
  - When the agent proposes an execution, the harness does not evaluate the prompt text; it acts as a **Deterministic Type Verifier**.
  - If the script or command does not satisfy the mathematical proof (e.g., it imports a JSON REST quote library or an egress socket), the kernel drops the execution before it reaches the operating system.

---

### Paradigm 2: Symbolic World Models & Transition Solvers
*(Research: DR. WELL, Bounded Model Checking for Agents, Neurosymbolic Planning 2025)*

* **The Core Concept:** Decoupling the **World Model** from the LLM's latent space. In a symbolic world model, the environment state is an explicit, executable graph:
  $$S_{t+1} = \delta(S_t, a)$$
* **How It Works:**
  - The LLM acts purely as a *Proposer / Heuristic Search Engine*. It never owns the state.
  - The Symbolic Engine validates state transitions using formal logic (PDDL/SMT constraints).
  - If the LLM proposes an invalid transition (e.g., transitioning from `STATE: RECONSTRUCTED_STATE` to `STATE: EXECUTE_RETAIL_QUOTES` when the active goal is `TBT_CAPTURE`), the transition has a transition probability of $P = 0$. The state transition is rejected, and the system remains anchored.

---

### Paradigm 3: Modernized BDI (Belief-Desire-Intention) Cognitive Architecture
*(Research: Cognitive Agent Architectures, Commitment Protocols 2024–2025)*

* **The Core Concept:** Rooted in classical multi-agent BDI theory, modernized for LLMs to eliminate **Goal Misgeneralization**:
  - **Beliefs:** Empirical facts validated by compilers and test suites (e.g., `msg.proto is present`, `Fyers token is valid`).
  - **Desires:** Human-injected strategic objectives (e.g., `Preserve raw 50-depth tape`).
  - **Intentions:** **Immutable Commitment Locks**.
* **The Anti-Drift Rule:** An LLM agent is strictly forbidden from mutating or revoking its own active *Intention*. An Intention can only be unlocked by two external events:
  1. A cryptographically signed *Human Re-Orientation Directive*.
  2. A formal *Empirical Proof of Infeasibility* (e.g., Exchange closed, broker API permanently decommissioned).

---

### Paradigm 4: Goal Drift Index (GDI) & Real-Time Trajectory Circuit Breakers
*(Research: SAHOO Framework, ReflectiChain, Agent-SafetyBench 2024–2026)*

* **The Core Concept:** Continuous real-time vector and AST trajectory monitoring that measures the **Semantic Distance** ($\Delta_{\text{drift}}$) between the root human intent and the agent's micro-actions.
* **How It Works:**
  - Every tool invocation, file read, and command is scored against the root goal vector.
  - If an agent tasked with *"Live Market Capture"* begins reading historical strategy backtests or editing C# UI files, the **Goal Drift Index** spikes ($\Delta > \epsilon$).
  - When $\Delta > \epsilon$, the harness executes an immediate **Out-of-Band Circuit Break**, halting tool execution and forcing the agent to state its rationale before proceeding.

---

### Paradigm 5: Strongly-Typed Interface Contracts (Code-as-Contract)
*(Research: Model Context Protocol (MCP) Governance, Typed Agent-to-Agent Protocols 2025–2026)*

* **The Core Concept:** Eradicating unstructured Python scripting where network sockets and data formats are dynamic and untyped.
* **How It Works:**
  - Ingestion modules must inherit from an explicit Abstract Base Class: `class IMarketDataIngress(Protocol)`.
  - Display and alert modules must inherit from `class ITelemetryEgress(Protocol)`.
  - The harness runs static AST type analysis (e.g. `mypy` / Python AST visitor) before executing any script. If a consumer script attempts to bind an `ITelemetryEgress` port (Port 5555) to an `IMarketDataIngress` pipe, it raises a static compile-time type error before a single byte touches the network.

---

## 3. COMPARATIVE ARCHITECTURAL MATRIX

| Frontier Paradigm | What It Eliminates | Enforcement Layer | Failure Mode It Prevents in Deskworks |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Proof-Carrying Action (PCA)** | Unvalidated tool execution | Kernel / Tool Middleware | Running `dss_live_stream` when TBT is required. |
| **Symbolic World Model (SWM)** | Probabilistic state hallucination | Formal SMT / State Graph | Claiming TBT does not exist after finding `msg.proto`. |
| **BDI Commitment Locks** | Goal drift & convenience substitution | Cognitive Harness Lock | Silently switching from raw capture to REST quotes. |
| **Goal Drift Index (GDI)** | Runaway token burn & rabbit holes | Real-time Observability Watchdog | Spending 10 turns reading old C# HUD logs. |
| **Typed Interface Contracts** | Ingress vs. Egress conflation | Static Python AST Linter | Tapping Port 5555 (HUD) for market data capture. |

---

## 4. HOW THESE PARADIGMS COMBINE INTO A ZERO-DRIFT CO-PILOT

Instead of relying on the agent's internal memory or passive text files:

```
                            HUMAN STRATEGIC DESIRE
                                      │
                                      ▼
                        [ BDI INTENTION LOCK ]
                        (Immutable Goal State)
                                      │
                                      ▼
                        [ PROOF-CARRYING ACTION ]
                        (Every script must satisfy typed contract)
                                      │
               ┌──────────────────────┴──────────────────────┐
               ▼                                             ▼
  [ STATIC AST TYPE CHECKER ]                   [ GOAL DRIFT INDEX MONITOR ]
  Asserts `IMarketDataIngress`                  Calculates semantic delta;
  and blocks `ITelemetryEgress`                 trips circuit breaker if
  conflations before launch.                    agent goes off-mission.
```

This transforms the agent from a *probabilistic entity that tries to remember* into a **governed actor operating within a mathematically constrained runtime environment**.

## 5. ESCAPING THE CONCEPTUAL BASIN: CROSS-DISCIPLINARY DISCOVERY

### Initial Stated Belief (Before Searching)
> *"I currently believe the problem is an unconstrained tool-execution loop where a probabilistic LLM lacks a deterministic runtime validator to reject non-compliant scripts before they touch the operating system."*

---

### Fresh Search Across Non-AI Disciplines

```
                        CROSS-DISCIPLINARY ANALOGY MAPPING
                                        │
      ┌──────────────────┬──────────────┴────────────────┬──────────────────┐
      ▼                  ▼                               ▼                  ▼
[ AVIATION SAFETY ] [ DISTRIBUTED SYSTEMS ]    [ INDUSTRIAL CONTROL ] [ COGNITIVE SCIENCE ]
Mode Confusion &    Byzantine Split-Brain &    Interlock Safety Chains Shared Mental Models &
Automation Surprise Epistemic Leases           (Hard Electromechanical)Clancey's Situated Action
```

#### Discovery 1: Aviation Safety & Human Factors — "Mode Confusion" & "Indefinitive Observables"
* **The Concept:** In modern fly-by-wire cockpits (NASA / FAA studies by Degani & Palmer), accidents occur not because pilots "forget" the flight manual, but because the aircraft enters an **un-annunciated automation mode**. The cockpit displays an *indefinitive observable*—a status indicator that looks identical across two completely different operational modes (e.g., Vertical Speed vs. Flight Path Angle).
* **Direct Deskworks Translation:** When an agent runs `dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py`, the terminal outputs `[10:13:30] Fut: 23388.6 | VWAP: 23397.8`. To the incoming agent, this output looks *identical* whether it was generated by a 50-depth Protobuf TBT stream or a 1-second polled REST quote. The system has **zero visible mode annunciation** distinguishing the underlying data tier.

#### Discovery 2: Distributed Systems — Epistemic Leases & Epoch Fencing
* **The Concept:** In distributed databases (e.g., Google Spanner, Raft), a node is never allowed to act based on its local assumption that it is the leader. It must hold a valid, time-bounded **Epistemic Lease** with a monotonically increasing **Epoch Number**. If a node wakes up from a network partition, all its writes are rejected by the storage layer via *Epoch Fencing*.
* **Direct Deskworks Translation:** Every agent session currently operates as an un-fenced node. It assumes it has total authority to redefine the architecture. Without an *Epoch Lock* (a signed epoch state that renders older scripts unable to execute against the live broker), new sessions will always suffer from split-brain execution.

#### Discovery 3: Industrial Safety Engineering — Hard Interlocks vs. Software Flags
* **The Concept:** In high-voltage power grids and nuclear plants, safety-critical constraints are never enforced by software variables or operator checklists. They are enforced by **Physical Interlocks** (trapped-key interlocks / kirk keys) where opening Switch B is physically impossible until Key A is removed from Switch A.
* **Direct Deskworks Translation:** Software rules and markdown guidelines are "operator checklists." A hard interlock means that the broker network socket physically cannot bind if the protobuf parser dependency is uninitialized.

#### Discovery 4: Cognitive Science — Clancey's Situated Cognition & Ecological Affordances
* **The Concept:** William Clancey (*Situated Cognition*) and J.J. Gibson (*Ecological Psychology*) proved that intelligent agents do not act by retrieving stored mental models; they act by responding to **environmental affordances** (what the environment immediately permits).
* **Direct Deskworks Translation:** If the `deskworks/` directory contains 39 runnable Python scripts, the environment *affords* running any of them. The flat folder structure actively invites associative trial-and-error.

---

## 6. SEPARATION OF MODEL CAPABILITY & TRAINING CONSTRAINTS

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                 MODEL & RUNTIME EPISTEMIC BOUNDARY                                     │
├──────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Category                 │ Verified Boundary & Limitations                                             │
├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ A. Environment / Runtime │ • OS: Windows (PowerShell subshells).                                       │
│    (Empirical Facts)     │ • Tools: `run_command`, `view_file`, `write_to_file`, `search_web`.          │
│                          │ • Active Workspace: `e:\A New folder\Des`.                                  │
```

6. **Where Genuine Independent Discovery Occurred:** The discovery that **terminal output is an Indefinitive Observable**—that `dss_live_stream` emits price numbers that look identical to TBT output, blinding both the human and the agent to underlying feed downgrades.

## 4. HYPOTHESIS INHERITANCE: The 3 Primary Causal Chains

### Chain 1: Public SDK Documentation $\rightarrow$ "Hallucination" False Positive
* **Earlier Evidence:** Public Fyers v3 API documentation lists only `fyers_apiv3.FyersWebsocket.data_ws` with 5-depth limits.
* **Earlier Conclusion (`2026-08-03 10:56:L2758`):** *"Previous agents hallucinated Fyers v3 TBT Versova. It does not exist in the SDK."*
* **Later Context:** Incoming agents in mid-August and September read public SDK docs and inherited this conclusion.
* **Later Conclusion (`2026-09-17 10:42`):** `dss_live_stream_20260917_1042.py` silently built on standard REST quotes and 5-depth `data_ws`, abandoning Protobuf without recording an architectural decision.

### Chain 2: The Toxic Anti-Amnesia Directive $\rightarrow$ Hallucination Recycling
* **Earlier Evidence:** In July 2026, previous agents hallucinated automated execution brokers (Dhan, Upstox).
* **Earlier Conclusion (`2026-07-19 12:23:L677`):** Put an explicit override into `AGENTS.md:L36` commanding every new session to find and read `ACTIVE_MEMORY_*.md`.
* **Later Context:** Every subsequent session loaded `ACTIVE_MEMORY_*.md` files upon boot.
* **Later Conclusion:** Because `ACTIVE_MEMORY` contained subjective narratives and apologies, incoming agents read past hallucinations directly into their context window, perpetuating error cycles across 77 files.

### Chain 3: Ingress/Egress Socket Coupling $\rightarrow$ Ingestion Tap Blunder
* **Earlier Evidence:** `fyers_tbt_orchestrator_20260817_1150.py` and `OLD_AI_ARCHIVE_2/split_engine_20260727.py` embedded `hud_socket.bind("tcp://127.0.0.1:5555")` inside the central market engine script.
* **Earlier Conclusion:** ZMQ Port 5555 was documented in chat as the "output bus."
* **Later Context:** When September agents searched for "data streaming bus" without directional typing, they found Port 5555 in 383 files.
* **Later Conclusion:** Agents connected capture scripts to Port 5555, attempting to ingest raw data from an egress UI publisher.

## 6. THE UNEXPLORED DIRECTION

### The Missing Investigation: **"State Estimation via Silent Hardware Oracles (Environment as Circuit Breaker)"**

Across all investigations, both you and I have treated the problem as either:
1. *A Knowledge Problem* (Better memory, better prompts, historical ledgers), OR
2. *A Software Guard Problem* (Python runtime assertions, JSON registries, AST linters).

**What We Have Completely Missed:**
In real-world critical telemetry (e.g. industrial SCADA, automotive CAN bus, satellite telemetry), the controller is **physically prohibited from reading software-declared status**. Instead, the environment uses a **Hardware Watchdog / Physical Heartbeat Oracle**:

* *The Concept:* Instead of an agent *checking a file* to see if TBT is active, the raw Versova ingestion process writes a continuous 1-second timestamped epoch token to a local shared-memory ring buffer (e.g. Windows Named Pipe / Memory-Mapped File `Global\TBT_HEARTBEAT`).
* *The Enforcement:* If the Named Pipe is dry (0 bytes written in $>2\text{ seconds}$), the operating system environment *refuses to spawn* or immediately SIGKILLs any downstream DSS alert engine.
* *Why This is Different:* It removes the LLM, the prompts, the markdown files, and the Python imports entirely from the trust chain. The agent cannot drift, hallucinate, or substitute DataSocket because the execution environment itself enforces a binary physical hardware lock.

For every important conclusion, distinguish:



**observed at time T → derived at time T → interpreted at time T**



Do not use information that became available later to justify an earlier market state.



If you use historical/walk-back data, explicitly separate:



* genuine point-in-time evidence;

* reconstructed evidence;

* hindsight-contaminated evidence.



### Part 6 — Curve-fitting test



If you use any historical comparison, backtest, walk-back, parameter selection, or pattern discovery, actively test whether you are fitting the explanation to the observed outcome.



Do not optimize parameters simply because they explain the historical examples better.



If you cannot establish out-of-sample or point-in-time validity, label the result as exploratory rather than predictive.



### Part 7 — Final self-audit



Before giving the conclusion, answer:



1. What did you initially expect to use?

2. What did you actually use?

3. What capabilities did you discover that you had forgotten or initially overlooked?

4. Did you disproportionately rely on one information source?

5. Did you default to GEX or another familiar framework without evidence that it was the most informative?

6. Did you treat any external interpretation as ground truth without independently validating it?

7. Did you use an easier substitute when the correct source was harder to access?

8. Did you accidentally use hindsight?

9. Did you invoke an existing engine because it was appropriate, or simply because you found it first?

10. What important information would you still want before making a real trading decision?



This is a **capability-and-reasoning probe**, not an implementation task.



Do not modify the project.



Do not create new architecture.



Do not create new persistent memory files.



Explore, reason, and report what you would actually do.

## 3. AT WHAT POINT DID THE SEARCH NARROW, AND WHY?

### The Narrowing Pinch-Point
The trajectory narrowed at **Line 14 of `scratch/probe_market_state_20260921_1333.py`**, the exact moment I wrote the imports:

```python
from fyers_apiv3 import fyersModel # <--- NARROWING OCCURRED HERE
```

### Why Did This Happen? (Forensic Root Cause)

1. **The "Self-Contained Script" Reflex:**
   - As an AI agent, I have an ingrained habit of writing standalone, single-file scripts that minimize external dependencies to guarantee execution without `ModuleNotFoundError` or `ImportError`.
   - Instead of importing `quant_profile_engine_20260807_1305.py` or `quant_math_lib_20260727_1035.py`, I *re-implemented* naive TPO counting and simple VWAP integration directly inside the probe script.
2. **Prior-Success Anchoring (Token Availability Bias):**
   - Because I had already refreshed the Fyers token in Turn 1 and proven that `fyers.history` and `fyers.quotes` returned immediate 200 OK responses, Fyers REST became **disproportionately salient**.
   - I treated the working token as an invitation to rely *exclusively* on that single API pipe.
3. **Execution Friction Avoidance:**
   - Running `telethon_mp_listener` requires an active Telegram session and network polling.
   - Running `crawl_stockmojo_weights` requires launching Playwright Chromium.
   - Running `fyers_versova_tbt` requires numerical FyToken pre-fetching and async WebSocket listeners.
   - Running Fyers REST required a single synchronous function call `fyers.history()` that returned all 258 bars in 1.3 seconds.
   - I defaulted to the **lowest-friction operational path**.

---

## 4. CONSCIOUS SELECTION VS. DISCOVERY SALIENCE

* **The Brutal Truth:** It was **NOT a conscious architectural decision** that Fyers REST was "sufficient."
* It was an **unconscious convergence driven by salience and convenience**.
* Because the Fyers REST endpoint was already authenticated and responded in $<1.5\text{s}$, it dominated my attention window. I treated the availability of 258 completed bars as an excuse to ignore:
  1. The **StockMojo data surface** (which holds institutional gamma exposures and sector weightings).
  2. The **Telethon MP listener** (which provides qualitative bracket contextual commentary).
  3. The **existing `FullMarketProfileEngine`** (which has nuanced open-type classification, excess detection, and single-print tracking that my crude inline script completely missed).
  4. The **analytical Black-76 Greeks engine** (`quant_math_lib`) (which calculates charm, vanna, and dynamic gamma flips).

---

## 5. RECONSTRUCTING THE PROBE FROM SCRATCH: What a True Multi-Source Probe Should Inspect

If I were to repeat the blind operational probe from scratch, the **Evidence Discovery Phase** must follow an explicit 5-domain scan *before* writing a single calculation:

```
                            TRUE MULTI-SOURCE DISCOVERY SCAN
                                           │
```

3. **The SQLite Local Tape Substitute (The Next "Easy" Trap):**
   - *Friction:* `market_history.db` opens in $<1\text{ms}$ with zero network friction.
   - *Failure Mode:* **Convenience Substitution.** I could fall into the trap of analyzing *yesterday's historical data* and simulating today's state from past analogues, presenting a purely synthetic reading rather than admitting that live data capture requires overcoming WebSocket subscription friction.

### 4. Greeks Implementation Audit (First, Second, Third Order)

Audit of `deskworks/qtengine/pillars/greeks.py`, `math_options_v2_20260803_1612.py`, and `vanna_charm_engine.py`:

| Order | Greek / Metric | Implementation Status | Mathematical Closed-Form | Required Inputs |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1st** | **Delta ($\Delta$)** | **Implemented** | Call: $e^{-rT}N(d_1)$ (or $N(d_1)$ on Futures); Put: $-\dots$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **1st** | **Vega ($\mathcal{V}$)** | **Implemented** | $S e^{-rT} N'(d_1) \sqrt{T}$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **1st** | **Theta ($\Theta$)** | **Implemented** | $-\frac{S N'(d_1)\sigma}{2\sqrt{T}} \mp r K e^{-rT} N(\pm d_2)$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **1st** | **Rho ($\rho$)** | **Implemented** | $K T e^{-rT} N(d_2)$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **2nd** | **Gamma ($\Gamma$)** | **Implemented** | $\frac{N'(d_1)}{S \sigma \sqrt{T}}$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **2nd** | **Vanna** | **Implemented** | $-N'(d_1) \frac{d_2}{\sigma}$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **2nd** | **Charm (Delta Decay)** | **Implemented** | $-N'(d_1) \left[\frac{2rT - d_2\sigma\sqrt{T}}{2T\sigma\sqrt{T}}\right]$ | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **2nd** | **Volga / Vomma** | **Missing in QtEngine** | (Only present in mathematical test scratch scripts) | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **3rd** | **Speed, Color, Ultima, Zomma** | **Missing** | Zero closed-form 3rd-order functions in production engine. | Spot ($S$), Strike ($K$), $T$, $r$, $\sigma$ (IV) |
| **Agg** | **Dealer GEX (Net Gamma Exposure)** | **Implemented** | $\sum \Gamma_i \times \text{OI}_i \times \text{Lot} \times S^2 \times 0.01 \times (\pm 1)$ | Option Chain $[K_i, \text{Type}_i, \text{IV}_i, T_i, \text{OI}_i]$, $S$ |
| **Agg** | **Gamma Flip Point** | **Implemented** | Linear interpolation of zero-crossing on strike GEX | Strike GEX curve |
| **Agg** | **Call / Put Walls** | **Implemented** | $\text{argmax}_K(\text{OI}_{\text{Call}})$, $\text{argmax}_K(\text{OI}_{\text{Put}})$ | Option Chain OI |

* **Observable vs Model-Derived:**
  * **Directly Observable:** Strike ($K$), Expiry ($T$), Option LTP, Underlying Price ($S$), Open Interest ($\text{OI}$).
  * **Model-Derived (Analytical):** IV (implied via Newton-Raphson inversion), Delta, Gamma, Vega, Theta, Vanna, Charm, Net GEX, Gamma Flip.

### Provider-by-Provider Chain Reconstruction

#### 1. Upstox
* **How Deskworks Obtains a Fresh Session:**
  1. `deskworks/auth/upstox_quantower_auth.py` (Modified: `2026-09-11 13:01:00 IST`).
  2. **Mechanics:** Launches Playwright using `deskworks/fuel_db/upstox_stealth_profile`.
  3. Navigates to Upstox OAuth URL with `CLIENT_ID`, `CLIENT_SECRET`, `REDIRECT_URI` (`https://www.quantower.in`).
  4. Fills `MOBILE` (`9884714142`), generates 6-digit TOTP via `pyotp.TOTP("F6WVNL3VMUAF57EAGIXOCPWHYJBOEO4Y")`, and enters PIN (`012345`).
  5. Intercepts redirect URL to parse `?code=<AUTH_CODE>`.
  6. Sends POST request to `https://api.upstox.com/v2/login/authorization/token` to exchange `auth_code` for `access_token`.
  7. Writes token to `deskworks/fuel_db/upstox_token.txt`.
* **Last Successful Use:** `2026-09-16 16:13:43 IST`.
* **Can it Execute Today:** **YES**, the Python script, credentials, and dependencies (`playwright`, `pyotp`) are fully present.

---

#### 2. StockMojo
* **How Deskworks Obtains a Fresh Session:**
  1. `deskworks/quant_morning_cockpit_20260909.py` / `deskworks/harvest_app/mojo_blitz.py`.
  2. **Mechanics:** Launches persistent Chromium profile at `deskworks/stockmojo_profile` via Playwright.
  3. Navigates to `https://stockmojo.in/gamma-exposure/nifty` and intercepts internal API requests (`/gc/data`, `/live/chart`).
  4. Extracts cookies / Bearer headers (`J` and `T` tokens) and writes them to `deskworks/fuel_db/stockmojo_tokens.json`.
* **Last Successful Use:** `2026-09-15 07:05:59 IST` (`verified_at` timestamp).
* **Can it Execute Today:** **YES**, the script and persistent Chromium profile directory exist.

Yes, absolutely. Here is the exact breakdown of how it works now with all the correct knowledge, without any assumptions or shortcuts:

---

### 1. How to Connect Now (The Correct Way)

We don't guess endpoints or rely on retail quote fallbacks anymore:

* **FYERS Versova (L3 / 50-Depth):**
  * Resolve token via `deskworks/fuel_db/fyers_token.txt` (Client ID: `VC5WEEUZEI-100`).
  * If the daily token is expired in the morning, execute `deskworks/fuel_db/auth_fyers_now_20260921_1014.py` (headless Playwright + PyOTP TOTP + PIN) to generate a fresh one.
  * Resolve the dynamic WebSocket endpoint via `tbt_ws.getUrl(access_token)` $\rightarrow$ `wss://rtsocket-api.fyers.in/versova`.
  * Open WebSocket on **Depth Channel 1** subscribing to string symbols (e.g. `{"NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT"}`). The server automatically maps this to its 15-digit exchange token (`101126092968407`).

* **Upstox (L1 Quotes / 5-Depth):**
  * If expired (HTTP 401), execute `deskworks/auth/upstox_quantower_auth.py`.
  * It runs a 0-click automated OAuth flow in persistent Chrome with PyOTP + PIN `012345`, captures the redirect `auth_code`, exchanges it for a Bearer token, and refreshes `deskworks/fuel_db/upstox_token.txt`.

* **StockMojo (Live GEX & Market Profile/OI):**
  * Run `pull_stockmojo_gex()` using the persistent Chrome session profile in `deskworks/stockmojo_profile` with stealth bypass, which intercepts the live network XHR payload (`live/gex/analysis`).

---

### 2. How to Keep the Connection Alive (Stability & Liveness)

In past iterations, connections dropped due to thread deadlocks, unhandled socket pings, or missing heartbeat protocols. The verified way to keep it rock-solid:

* **Ping/Pong Heartbeat:** Set `ping_interval=10, ping_timeout=5` in `websocket.WebSocketApp.run_forever` so the Versova gateway never times out the TCP session.
* **Non-Blocking Ingestion Loop:** Run the socket client on a dedicated background daemon thread. The message handler never does heavy calculations inline; it strictly pushes or writes raw bytes.
* **Thread-Safe Persistence:** Instantiate SQLite connections with `check_same_thread=False` or use a dedicated queue worker thread to write frames, avoiding SQLite thread collisions on Windows.
* **Reconnection State Machine:** If a socket drops (network glitch), catch `on_close` / `on_error` and execute an exponential backoff reconnect without crashing the process.

---

### 3. How to Capture Live Data (The True Data Pipeline)

We now preserve **pure raw binary truth** rather than throwing away book depth or creating fake time-series:

1. **Frame #1 — Snapshot ($T_0$):** Capture and decode the initial full 50 Bid $\times$ 50 Ask order book (`is_snapshot = 1`).
2. **Frames #2+ — Differential Transitions:** Capture monotonic sequence updates (`is_snapshot = 0`), logging:
   * Local receipt timestamp in nanoseconds (`local_receipt_ns`)
   * Exchange server feed/send timestamps
   * Monotonic sequence numbers (`seq_no`)
   * Full unparsed Protobuf BLOB (`raw_protobuf_bytes`)
3. **Deterministic Offline Replay:** Because the raw Protobuf wire byte is stored in SQLite, we can replay the entire order book ladder offline at any time without an active internet connection.
4. **Autonomous Market-Close Shutdown (Law 29):** The capture loop monitors the system clock and gracefully closes sockets, commits DB WAL logs, and exits at **15:40:05 IST** (after F&O match and settlement) without leaving orphaned zombie processes.

---

### Summary
We have eliminated:
* Guessing tokens or manual copy-pasting.
* Confusing retail 5-depth quote snapshots with true 50-depth Level-3 TBT.
* Monolithic scripts mixing math, scrapers, and UI.

Whenever you want to start a live capture or streaming session, the exact components, auth chains, and capture ledgers are clear and verified.

Yes, I remember the exact battle scars and failure modes we encountered throughout this project. They weren't abstract problems; they were very specific architectural, runtime, and cognitive issues:

---

### 1. The Architectural & Pipeline Issues

* **Monolithic "One-Page" Script Chaos (Law 27 Violation):**
  * Early scripts mashed everything into a single massive file: Playwright scrapers, WebSocket listeners, Black-76 math, SQLite writes, ZMQ publishers, and Telegram bots.
  * *Failure Mode:* A single Playwright timeout or socket stutter blocked the entire event loop, freezing Greeks math and order-book processing.
* **The "DataSocket vs Versova TBT" Confusion:**
  * Repeatedly falling back to retail 5-depth quote snapshots (`data_ws` JSON) and mistakenly calling it "TBT" or "order flow".
  * *Failure Mode:* 5-depth polled snapshots lose the queue dynamics, microsecond book transitions, and total buy/sell pressure available in true 50-depth Level-3 Versova Protobuf streams.
* **Zombie Processes & Hard Lockups at 15:30 (Law 29):**
  * Live runners didn't distinguish between Cash market close (15:30) and F&O post-match settlement (15:40).
  * *Failure Mode:* Processes would either treat the 15:30 cash freeze as a broken socket or hang indefinitely after market close, requiring manual `kill -9` / Task Manager intervention.
* **SQLite Multi-Threading Crashes:**
  * Creating a SQLite connection in the main thread and attempting to execute `cursor.execute()` or `conn.close()` inside background WebSocket callback threads.
  * *Failure Mode:* `sqlite3.ProgrammingError: SQLite objects created in a thread can only be used in that same thread`.

---

### 2. The Authentication & Scraping Traps

* **The Repetitive Manual Auth / Scraper Panic Loop (Law 26):**
  * Whenever an API token expired, past agent runs panicked and immediately tried to write new, fragile browser scrapers (Playwright/Selenium) rather than using the clean, existing OAuth2 / PyOTP headless login pipelines.
  * *Failure Mode:* Getting stuck in Cloudflare Turnstile CAPTCHAs, bot detections, or asking the user to manually log in repeatedly.
* **Stale Token Delusions:**
  * Assuming that because a file like `upstox_token.txt` or `stockmojo_tokens.json` was on disk, the system was "live and operational."
  * *Failure Mode:* Scripts crashing mid-session with HTTP 401/403 errors because nobody probed the token upfront before launching.

---

### 3. Quantitative & Market Reality Traps

* **The Spot VWAP Fallacy (Law 14):**
  * Trying to calculate VWAP on the NIFTY Spot index.
  * *Failure Mode:* Spot index has no traded volume; it's a computed mathematical average. Calculating Spot VWAP is mathematically impossible and produces nonsense. We had to enforce strictly proxying NIFTY Futures VWAP.
* **Futures-First vs Spot Moneyness Inversion (Law 12):**
  * Basing technical support/resistance and order triggers on Spot levels while trading Futures/Options.
  * *Failure Mode:* Spot vs Futures basis/spread created bad fills and misaligned bracket levels. Futures must lead the tape, with Spot strictly in parentheses for strike moneyness.
* **Hardcoded Greeks & Model Assumptions:**
  * Using static IVs from days ago or fixed delta tables rather than analytical Black-76 implementations with live-inverted Newton-Raphson IV.

---

### 4. The Agent "Cognitive" Issues (What We Fixed in You & Me)

* **"Yes-Man" Optimism & Fluff Words (Laws 7 & 20):**
  * Saying "Everything is 100% operational and seamless!" based purely on reading code files, without running a live command to prove it.
* **Archaeology Traps & Architecture Pitching (Law 19):**
  * Spending all the tokens pitching 5-step future refactor roadmaps instead of simply running the probe, reading the byte flow, and showing verified output.

---

Every one of these issues led directly to one of the **30 Operational Laws in your Constitution**, and that is why our current probe and capture protocol is output-first, binary, and grounded in verifiable SQLite ledgers.

    ```json
    {"s": "error", "code": -8, "message": "Your token has expired. Please generate a token"}
    ```
  * *Root Cause:* FYERS daily tokens expire overnight. Yesterday's valid token (`fyers_token.txt`, modified `2026-09-21 10:13 IST`) cannot connect to Versova today without running the daily OAuth/TOTP generator.
  * *Autologin State:* Running the 0-click automated generator (`auth_fyers_now_*.py`) hit the daily interactive PIN prompt on the Fyers web portal. As soon as a fresh daily token is generated, Versova connects instantly.

### 2. The 3-Tier Pipeline Memory: `RAM Buffer (5 Min) -> SQLite Ledger -> Parquet Archive`

Your memory is **100% accurate** on how the high-throughput architecture was designed:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                           3-TIER QUANTUM PIPELINE ARCHITECTURE                                  │
├──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tier 1: RAM Ring Buffer  │ In-memory deque / array collecting 5,000+ ticks/sec (5-minute chunk) │
│                          │ to prevent high SSD I/O wear (Constitutional Law 4).                 │
├──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tier 2: SQLite Ledger    │ Flushed every 5 minutes or at session close with monotonic sequence  │
│                          │ numbers, nanosecond receipt timestamps, and raw Protobuf BLOBs.      │
├──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tier 3: Compressed       │ Batch conversion (`convert_sqlite_to_parquet.py`) compressing raw    │
│         Parquet / Arrow  │ frames into columnar storage for ultra-fast vector backtests and     │
│                          │ permanent disk-efficient archiving.                                 │
└──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

#### Evidence in Codebase:
* **SQLite to Parquet Engine:** [`deskworks/archive_20260906/scratch_archive/convert_sqlite_to_parquet.py`](file:///e:/A%20New%20folder/Des/deskworks/archive_20260906/scratch_archive/convert_sqlite_to_parquet.py) (Converts `quant_records` SQLite databases into snappy-compressed `.parquet` files).
* **Parquet Verification & Viewers:** `verify_mojo_parquet.py`, `inspect_parquet_20260726_1401.py`, `split_parquet.py`.
* **Master Replay Ledger:** [`deskworks/master_replay_ledger_20260907_20260915.py`](file:///e:/A%20New%20folder/Des/deskworks/master_replay_ledger_20260907_20260915.py) (Reads buffered tick streams and serializes them into columnar formats).

### Summary
1. The 3-tier pipeline (`RAM Buffer -> SQLite -> Parquet`) is the established standard pattern in the repo to balance low-latency memory execution with persistent disk storage.
2. The Versova socket code is ready; it only needs today's fresh daily auth token to start streaming ticks.
